    panic(err)

Fields

Field Type Required Description
alert_sources array Yes Which alert sources this route matches
condition_groups array Yes Filter: the condition groups that must be true for this route to fire
created_at string No When this alert route was created
enabled boolean Yes Whether this alert route is enabled
escalation_config object Yes Escalation Config
expressions array Yes The expressions used by bindings in this route
grouping_config object Yes Grouping Config
id string Yes Unique identifier for this alert route
incident_config object Yes Incident Config
is_private boolean Yes Whether this alert route is private.
message_config object Yes Message Config
name string Yes The name of this alert route, for the user's reference
owning_team_ids array No IDs of teams that own this alert route
updated_at string No When this alert route was last updated
version integer Yes The version of this alert route

create

POST /v2/alert_routes

Operation ID: Alert Routes V2#Create.

Authentication: bearer token (BearerAuth).

Example request body:

{
  "alert_sources": [
    {
      "alert_source_id": "01FCNDV6P870EA6S7TK1DSYDG0",
      "condition_groups": [
        {
          "conditions": [
            {
              "operation": "one_of",
              "param_bindings": [
                {
                  "array_value": [
                    {
                      "literal": "SEV123",
                      "reference": "incident.severity"
                    }
                  ],
                  "value": {
                    "literal": "SEV123",
                    "reference": "incident.severity"
                  }
                }
              ],
              "subject": "incident.severity"
            }
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  ],
  "channel_config": [
    {

escalation_config.when_alert_joins_group object No
escalation_config.when_alert_joins_group.grace_period_seconds integer (int32) No How long to wait before escalating once an alert joins the group, in seconds. Only applies when mode is 'on_each_new_alert', and must be unset when mode is 'on_priority_increase'. Must be between 0 and 3600 (1 hour).
escalation_config.when_alert_joins_group.mode enum: on_priority_increase, on_each_new_alert Yes When a subsequent alert joins an existing group, when should we escalate again?

grouping_config object Yes
grouping_config.default object Yes
grouping_config.default.ai_enabled boolean No Use AI to group similar looking alerts. AI alert grouping can only group alerts that are attributed to the same team, so grouping keys must only contain the team alert attribute. Private alerts are not grouped unless AI incident access allows private incidents and alerts. Omit it on an update to keep the current value.
grouping_config.default.enabled boolean Yes Whether grouping is enabled
grouping_config.default.grouping_keys array of object No Which attributes should this alert route use to group alerts? Only set when grouping is enabled.
grouping_config.default.grouping_keys[].reference string Yes A reference to a property of the alert to group on
grouping_config.default.window_seconds integer (int32) No How long the grouping window is, in seconds. Must be between 60 (1 minute) and 172800 (48 hours). Only set when grouping is enabled.
grouping_config.default.window_type enum: rolling, fixed No Controls how the grouping window behaves. 'rolling' keeps the window open for window_seconds after the most recent alert, so the group stays open as long as alerts keep arriving. 'fixed' opens the window when the first alert arrives and always closes window_seconds later, regardless of any subsequent alerts. Only set when grouping is enabled.
incident_config object Yes
incident_config.auto_decline_enabled boolean No Should triage incidents be declined when alerts are resolved? Required when incident creation is enabled, and must be unset otherwise.
incident_config.condition_groups array of object No What condition groups must be true for this alert route to create an incident? Only set when incident creation is enabled.

Private alerts are not grouped unless AI incident access allows private incidents and alerts. Omit it on an update to keep the current value.

alert_route.grouping_config.default.window_type enum: rolling, fixed No Controls how the grouping window behaves. 'rolling' keeps the window open for window_seconds after the most recent alert, so the group stays open as long as alerts keep arriving. 'fixed' opens the window when the first alert arrives and always closes window_seconds later, regardless of any subsequent alerts. Only set when grouping is enabled.

list

GET /v2/alert_routes

Operation ID: Alert Routes V2#List.

Authentication: bearer token (BearerAuth).

Parameters

Parameter In Type Required Description
page_size query integer (int64) Yes Number of alert routes to return per page
after query string No The ID of the last alert route on the previous page

Responses

200: OK response

application/json

Property Type Required Description
alert_routes array of object Yes
alert_routes[].enabled boolean Yes Whether this alert route is enabled or not
alert_routes[].id string Yes Unique identifier for this alert route config
alert_routes[].name string Yes The name of this alert route config, for the user's reference
pagination_meta object Yes
pagination_meta.after string No If provided, pass this as the 'after' param to load the next page
pagination_meta.page_size integer (int64) Yes What was the maximum number of results requested

Example response:

{
  "alert_routes": [
    {
      "enabled": false,
      "id": "01FCNDV6P870EA6S7TK1DSYDG0",
      "name": "Production incidents"
    }
  ],
  "pagination_meta": {
    "after": "01FCNDV6P870EA6S7TK1DSYDG0",
    "page_size": 25
  }
}

GET /v3/alert_routes

Operation ID: Alert Routes V3#List.

Authentication: bearer token (BearerAuth).

Parameters

Parameter In Type Required Description
page_size query integer (int64) No Number of alert routes to return per page
after query string No The ID of the last alert route on the previous page

Responses

200: OK response

application/json

Property Type Required Description
alert_routes array of object Yes
alert_routes[].enabled boolean Yes Whether this alert route is enabled or not
alert_routes[].id string Yes Unique identifier for this alert route config
alert_routes[].name string Yes The name of this alert route config, for the user's reference
pagination_meta object Yes
pagination_meta.after string No If provided, pass this as the 'after' param to load the next page
pagination_meta.page_size integer (int64) Yes What was the maximum number of results requested

Example response:

{
  "alert_routes": [
    {
      "enabled": false,
      "id": "01FCNDV6P870EA6S7TK1DSYDG0",
      "name": "Production incidents"
    }
  ],
  "pagination_meta": {
    "after": "01FCNDV6P870EA6S7TK1DSYDG0",
    "page_size": 25
  }
}
400: invalid_request_error: There was a problem with the request, like a missing header or querying a resource that doesn't exist

load

GET /v2/alert_routes/{id}

Operation ID: Alert Routes V2#Show.

Authentication: bearer token (BearerAuth).

Parameters

Parameter In Type Required Description
id path string Yes Unique identifier of the alert route

Responses

200: OK response

application/json

Property Type Required Description
alert_route object Yes
alert_route.alert_sources array of object Yes Which alert sources should this alert route match?
alert_route.alert_sources[].alert_source_id string Yes The alert source ID that will match for the route
alert_route.alert_sources[].condition_groups array of object Yes What conditions should alerts from this source meet to be included in this alert route?
alert_route.alert_sources[].condition_groups[].conditions array of object Yes All conditions in this list must be satisfied for the group to be satisfied
alert_route.alert_sources[].condition_groups[].conditions[].operation object Yes
alert_route.alert_sources[].condition_groups[].conditions[].param_bindings array of object Yes Bindings for the operation parameters
alert_route.alert_sources[].condition_groups[].conditions[].subject object Yes
alert_route.channel_config array of object Yes The channel configuration for this alert route
alert_route.channel_config[].condition_groups array of object Yes The conditions that must be met for this channel config to be used
alert_route.channel_config[].condition_groups[].conditions array of object Yes All conditions in this list must be satisfied for the group to be satisfied
alert_route.channel_config[].condition_groups[].conditions[].operation object Yes
alert_route.channel_config[].condition_groups[].conditions[].param_bindings array of object Yes Bindings for the operation parameters
alert_route.channel_config[].condition_groups[].conditions[].subject object Yes
alert_route.channel_config[].ms_teams_targets object No
alert_route.channel_config[].ms_teams_targets.binding object Yes
alert_route.channel_config[].ms_teams_targets.binding.array_value array of object No If array_value is set, this helps render the values
alert_route.channel_config[].ms_teams_targets.binding.value object No
alert_route.channel_config[].ms_teams_targets.channel_visibility string Yes The visibility of the channel
alert_route.channel_config[].slack_targets object No
alert_route.channel_config[].slack_targets.binding object Yes
alert_route.channel_config[].slack_targets.binding.array_value array of object No If array_value is set, this helps render the values
alert_route.channel_config[].slack_targets.binding.value object No
alert_route.channel_config[].slack_targets.channel_visibility string Yes The visibility of the channel
alert_route.condition_groups array of object Yes What condition groups must be true for this alert route to fire?
alert_route.condition_groups[].conditions array of object Yes All conditions in this list must be satisfied for the group to be satisfied
alert_route.condition_groups[].conditions[].operation object Yes
alert_route.condition_groups[].conditions[].operation.label string Yes Human readable label to be displayed for user to select
alert_route.condition_groups[].conditions[].operation.value string Yes Unique identifier for this option

400: invalid_request_error: There was a problem with the request, like a missing header or querying a resource that doesn't exist

application/json

Property Type Required Description
debug object No
debug.message string Yes Original internal error message
debug.stacktrace array of string Yes Stacktrace of the error, if applicable
errors array of object Yes List of errors that caused this request to fail
errors[].code string Yes Machine-readable identifier for this specific error
errors[].message string Yes Human readable description of the error
errors[].metadata object No Additional metadata about the error, keyed by a string identifier
errors[].source object No
errors[].source.field string Yes Field name that is the source of the error
errors[].source.pointer string Yes JSON pointer to the request field that is the source of the error
rate_limit object No
rate_limit.limit integer (int64) Yes The maximum number of requests that the consumer is permitted to make per minute
rate_limit.name string Yes Which rate limit was exceeded
rate_limit.remaining integer (int64) Yes The number of requests remaining in the current rate limit window
rate_limit.retry_after string Yes When the client can retry, as an RFC3339 timestamp in UTC. Prefer the Retry-After response header, which carries the same instant as a number of seconds
request_id string Yes Unique identifier of the request
status integer (int64) Yes HTTP status of the response
type enum: invalid_request_error, authentication_error, resource_forbidden, not_found, not_acceptable, method_not_allowed, ... Yes Machine-readable identifier for the general category of error
401: authentication_error: Authentication failed

application/json

Property Type Required Description
debug object No
debug.message string Yes Original internal error message

errors array of object Yes List of errors that caused this request to fail

errors[].message string Yes Human readable description of the error
errors[].metadata object No Additional metadata about the error, keyed by a string identifier
errors[].source object No
errors[].source.field string Yes Field name that is the source of the error
errors[].source.pointer string Yes JSON pointer to the request field that is the source of the error
rate_limit object No
rate_limit.limit integer (int64) Yes The maximum number of requests that the consumer is permitted to make per minute
rate_limit.name string Yes Which rate limit was exceeded
rate_limit.remaining integer (int64) Yes The number of requests remaining in the current rate limit window
rate_limit.retry_after string Yes When the client can retry, as an RFC3339 timestamp in UTC. Prefer the Retry-After response header, which carries the same instant as a number of seconds
request_id string Yes Unique identifier of the request
status integer (int64) Yes HTTP status of the response
type enum: invalid_request_error, authentication_error, resource_forbidden, not_found, not_acceptable, method_not_allowed, ... Yes Machine-readable identifier for the general category of error
403: resource_forbidden: Access to this resource is forbidden for the authenticated user

application/json

Property Type Required Description

GET /v3/alert_routes/{id}

Operation ID: Alert Routes V3#Show.

Authentication: bearer token (BearerAuth).

Parameters

Parameter In Type Required Description
id path string Yes Unique identifier of the alert route

Responses

200: OK response

alert_route.grouping_config.default.ai_enabled boolean No Use AI to group similar looking alerts. AI alert grouping can only group alerts that are attributed to the same team, so grouping keys must only contain the team alert attribute. Private alerts are not grouped unless AI incident access allows private incidents and alerts. Omit it on an update to keep the current value.
alert_route.grouping_config.default.enabled boolean Yes Whether grouping is enabled

alert_route.grouping_config.default.window_seconds integer (int32) No How long the grouping window is, in seconds. Must be between 60 (1 minute) and 172800 (48 hours). Only set when grouping is enabled.
alert_route.grouping_config.default.window_type enum: rolling, fixed No Controls how the grouping window behaves. 'rolling' keeps the window open for window_seconds after the most recent alert, so the group stays open as long as alerts keep arriving. 'fixed' opens the window when the first alert arrives and always closes window_seconds later, regardless of any subsequent alerts. Only set when grouping is enabled.

grouping_config object Yes
grouping_config.default object Yes
grouping_config.default.ai_enabled boolean No Use AI to group similar looking alerts. AI alert grouping can only group alerts that are attributed to the same team, so grouping keys must only contain the team alert attribute. Private alerts are not grouped unless AI incident access allows private incidents and alerts. Omit it on an update to keep the current value.
grouping_config.default.enabled boolean Yes Whether grouping is enabled

grouping_config.default.window_seconds integer (int32) No How long the grouping window is, in seconds. Must be between 60 (1 minute) and 172800 (48 hours). Only set when grouping is enabled.
grouping_config.default.window_type enum: rolling, fixed No Controls how the grouping window behaves. 'rolling' keeps the window open for window_seconds after the most recent alert, so the group stays open as long as alerts keep arriving. 'fixed' opens the window when the first alert arrives and always closes window_seconds later, regardless of any subsequent alerts. Only set when grouping is enabled.

Private alerts are not grouped unless AI incident access allows private incidents and alerts. Omit it on an update to keep the current value.

alert_route.grouping_config.default.window_seconds integer (int32) No How long the grouping window is, in seconds. Must be between 60 (1 minute) and 172800 (48 hours). Only set when grouping is enabled.
alert_route.grouping_config.default.window_type enum: rolling, fixed No Controls how the grouping window behaves. 'rolling' keeps the window open for window_seconds after the most recent alert, so the group stays open as long as alerts keep arriving. 'fixed' opens the window when the first alert arrives and always closes window_seconds later, regardless of any subsequent alerts. Only set when grouping is enabled.
